Cix-iw iḥemmel-iyi imi sneɣ ad aruɣ taqbaylit.
Baba yuɣ-iyi-d iliboḍen i ccetwa-agi.
Lakul-nneɣ yebɛed ɣef uxxam-nneɣ s 8 ikilumitren.
Yewɛer ubrid ɣer lakul-nneɣ deg wass n udfel.
Ddiɣ ɣer ssuq akked baba iwakken ad d-naɣ akraren.
Amdakkel-iw saɛid yuker-iyi abalun-iw azeggaɣ.
Ččiɣ taḥbult ɣef sqef deg wass n sebɛa uɛecrin.
Nekk akked umdakkel-iw Ssaɛid nemḥemmal, meɛna nezga nettnaɣ ula ɣef ulac.
Nekk akked umdakkel-iw Saɛid nḥemmel di sin yiwet n teqcict.
Anta yebɣan ad turar yid-i amrar?
Anwa yebɣan ad yurar yid-i tuqna-tufra?
Anta yebɣan ad turar yid-i taɛelǧett?
Anwa yebɣan ad yurar yid-i abalun?
Sliɣ i Saɛid yettru mi t-yekkat baba-s.
Nek, baba, yemma akked weltma nruḥ ad d-nelqeḍ azemmur.
Yugi-iyi baba ad ṛuḥeɣ ɣer temda.
Yugi-iyi baba ad urareɣ lkarṭa.
Baba yebɣa ad iḥriceɣ di lakul.
Yugi-iyi baba ad keyyefeɣ.
Yugi-iyi baba ad cummeɣ.
Baba yebɣa ad iliɣ d aqcic imeɛnen.
Ddiɣ akked yemma ɣer tala ad d-nagwem.

A gma, ṛwaḥ ad nurar ileqqafen!
Jida tezga tḥekku-iyi-d timucuha rrif ukanun.
A yemma, uḍneɣ taɛebbuḍt-iw!
Yiwet n tikelt mi ɣliɣ ufiɣ duṛu.
Yenna-iyi-d baba belli agrud m'ara yeɣli yettaf duṛu.
Ḥareɣ melmi ara nuɣal ɣer lakul iwakken ad lseɣ icetiḍen-iw ijdiden.
Temceḍ-iyi yemma acebbub-iw, sakin tegga-iyi aqardun.

Karima iḥemmel-itt gma.

A yemma, anda-t uqrab-nni ara awiɣ ɣer lakul?

D amcum, Tom.

Ɣriɣ.
Neɣra.
Ad ɣreɣ.
Ɣriɣ deg uɣerbaz amezwaru.
Ur ɣriɣ ara deg uɣerbaz amezwaru.
Qqareɣ deg uɣerbaz amezwaru.
Ur qqareɣ ara deg uɣerbaz amezwaru.
Tom yesεa yelli-s teqqar deg uɣerbaz amezwaru.
Tom ur yesεi ara yelli-s i yeqqaren deg uɣerbaz amezwaru.
Yelli-s n Tom teqqar deg uɣerbaz amezwaru.
Yelli-s n Tom ur teqqar ara deg uɣerbaz amezwaru.
D yelli-s n Tom i yeqqaren deg uɣerbaz amezwaru, mačči d yelli-s n Mary.
Yelli-s n Tom mačči deg uɣerbaz amezwaru i teqqar, maca deg uɣerbaz alemmas.
Tom d Mary sεan aqcic d teqcict, aqcic-nni yeqqar deg tesdawit ma d taqcict-nni mazal-itt deg tesnawit.
Mary akked Tom.
Mary lwaḥid Tom.
Mary d Tom.
Arraw n Tom qqaren deg tesnawit.
Yessi-s n Tom qqarent deg tesnawit.
Yessi-s Tom ur qqarent ara deg tesdawit.
Tom, yas ass, yettawi arraw-is ɣer uɣerbaz.
Tom yexdem tameɣra ilmend n umulli-s, yeεreḍ-d akk imdukkal-is, yal yiwen d acu i as-d-yuwi d tarzeft.
Tom inced-d akk imdukkal-is ɣer tmeɣra i yexdem ilmend n umulli-ines.
Ula d Mary terza ɣer tmeɣra n umulli n Tom. Tuwi-as tamrilt n wureɣ d tarzeft.
Tom inced-d Mary ɣer tmɣra n umulli n mmi-s ameẓẓyan.
Tom inced-d Mary ɣer tmeɣra n umulli n mmi-s ameqqran.
Tom yeεreḍ Mary ɣer tmeɣra n umaẓuẓ-is.
Tom inced Mary ɣer tmeɣra n umulli n yelli-s.
Mary terza ɣer tmeɣra n umulli n yelli-s n Tom tuwi-as adlis ɣef yiɣersiwen d tarzeft.
Yelli-s n Tom tefreḥ s terzeft n Mary ilmend n umulli-s.
Tom yeεǧeb-as usefk n Mary, win i as-d-tefka ilmend n umulli-ines wis 40.
Asefk n Mary d tamrilt n wureɣ.
Tarzeft n Mary i yelli-s n Tom, d adlis ɣef yiɣersiwen.
Imdukkal n Tom merra uwin-as-d isefken.
Tom yebda-d aheyyi i tmeɣra ara yexdem ilmend n umulli-s wis 40, yettxemmim ad d-yenced akk imdukkal-is d twaculin-nsen.
Tefreḥ Mary imi tt-id-yenced Tom ɣer umulli-ines.
Tom yefreḥ nezzeh.
Tom yefreḥ d ayen din.
Tom yettu ur d-yencid ara Mary ɣef umulli-s, d ayen i tt-yeǧǧan terfa, iɣaḍ-itt lḥal.
Mary terfa ɣef Tom imi ur-tt-id-yencid ara ɣer tmeɣra n umulli-s.
Mary tɣil ad tt-inced Tom ɣer umulli-s, tekker tuɣ-as-d tarzeft ɣlayen, d tamrilt n wureɣ.
Tarzeft n Mary ɣlayet nezzeh yerna tecbeḥ d ayen din.
Tom yesnemmer Mary ɣef usefk i as-tuwi.
Tamurt n Lezzayer tezga-d deg ugafa n Tefriqt.
Leqbayel neɣ Iqbayliyen kif kif.
Leqbayel d imezdaɣ n tmurt n Leqbayel, ttmeslayen taqbaylit.
Tamurt n Leqbayel tettwassen s ccbaḥa n ugama-s, tesdukkel tizegzewt n yilel agrakal akked yidurar εlayen am Ǧerǧer d Lbabur.
Tom yerza-d ɣer tmurt n Leqbayel iwakken ad yesεeddi ussan-is n yimuras, yenna-d dakken teεǧeb-as nezzeh.
Tom yemmeslay-as i Mary ɣef wussan n yimuras i yesεedda deg tmurt n Leqbayel akked ccbaḥa-s yessewhamen, yettawin leεqel.
Mary tebɣa ad tẓer tamurt n Leqbayel.
Mary tebɣa ad d-terzu ɣer tmurt n Leqbayel deg yimuras i d-yetteddun.
Tamurt n Leqbayel tecbeḥ nezzeh deg tefsut, ad tafeḍ ijeǧǧigen deg yal tama, s yal ini: amellal, awraɣ, azegzaw, azeggaɣ, axuxi ...atg. Ad as-tiniḍ d tafelwit n teklut.
Tafsut d yiwet gar tsemhuyin n useggas.
Yeddem tiḥdert n uɣrum, yefka-as-tt i yiwen n umattar.

Yiwen n umattar ibded ɣef umnar n tewwirt n uxxam n yiwet n twacult tamrekantit, yessuter seg-sen tin n Rebbi.
Argaz yessuter seg tmeṭṭut-is ad as-d-tawi aɣrum d zzit iwakken ad as-ten-yefk i tmattart i yellan deg berra.

Tom yessen ad yurar ddabex n uḍar.
Tom d amyurar ifazen deg ddabex n uḍar.
Tom yetturar ddabex n uḍar yal taggara n ddurt.
Tom ur yessin ara ad yurar ddabex n uḍar.
Tom ur yetturar ara ddabex n uḍar.
Mary tḥemmel ddabex n uḍar.
Mary tessen ad turar ddabex n uḍar.
Mary ur tḥemmel ara ddabex n uḍar.
Mary tetturar ddabex n uḍar ɣer terbaεt n tesdawit ideg teqqar.
Mary ur tetturar ara ddabex n uḍar, acku ur t-tḥemmel ara.
Mary tḥemmel ad twali Tom yetturar ddabex n uḍar deg unnar n tɣiwant.
Tom yebɣa ad yurar ɣer terbaεt-nni i yuwin taqbuct n ddabex n uḍar tikkelt iεeddan.
Tom ur iḥemmel ara ad iwali timliliyin n ddabex n uḍar.
Tom d Mary ḥemmlen ad uraren ddabex n uḍar.
Tom d Mary tturaren ddabex n uḍar yal taggara n yimalas.
Yenna-as Tom i Mary dakken yetturar ddabex n uḍar yal ass.
Mary tḥemmel ddabex n uḍar maca ur tessin ara ad t-turar.
Mary leεmer turar ddabex n uḍar.
Mary werǧin turar ddabex n uḍar s wudem unsib.
Mary tɣil ur yessin ara Tom ad yurar ddabex n uḍar.

Yerra taɣawsa i bab-is.
Yerra tiɣawsiwin i bab-nsent.
Yerra tiɣawsiwin i bab-itsent.
Terra taccuyt neɣ tasilt i yimensi.
Yerra-d azal.
Yerra-d ayen akk i yečča.
Yerra-d ayen i yečča.
Mi d-yeffeɣ seg uɣerbaz, yerra srid s axxam.
Yerra-yi-d idrimen i as-reḍleɣ.
Yerra aḍar.
Yecceḍ, yerra aḍar.
Yerra-d awal.
Yerra-as awal i baba-s, mi i as-yessiwel.
Yerra-d ɣef tuttriwin akk i d-yeddan deg temsizzelt-nni.
Yerra-d akken ilaq ɣef usteqsi.
Yerra-d ɣef yiseqsiyen n ukayad akken iwata.
Awer yečč!
Qquren yiqerruyen-nsen.
Qquren yiqerra-nsen.
Sserwaten deg unebdu.
Yerwa tiyita.
Terwa leεqel.
Akken i yeqqar yinzi: Axxam mebla tamɣart am wurti mebla tadekkart.
Inzi-nniḍen yeqqar: Awal ma wezzil yelha, ma ɣezzif ad d-yernu kra.
Imezwura-nneɣ ǧǧan-d aṭas n yinzan neɣ lemtul i yesεan azal d ameqqran, yal yiwen s unamek-is d tegnit-is; yal inzi s temsirt-is.
Timucuha d agerruj ur nfennu, d timsirin n tudert.

D bu tissas yerna yeẓwer.
D mm tissas terna teẓwer.
D at tissas yerna ẓewren.
D sut tissas yerna ẓewrent.
Terra-t tmara ad yinig iwakken ad iḥelli aɣrum i twacult-is.
Zdi lqedd-ik temmuqleḍ ɣer sdat.
Zdi lqedd-im.

Azeṭṭa yettmaga s waṭas n wallalen am uyaẓil, aqerdac, asegru, tijebbadin...
Azeṭṭa yesεa azal d ameqqran ɣer teqbayliyin n zik.
Tegra yemma azeṭṭa, εawneɣ-tt deg-s.
Yemma d xalti grant azeṭṭa, azal n wayyur aya, ar tura urεad ifukk.
Tameṭṭut taqbaylit tzeṭṭ, s umata, deg yiḍ, imi imir-nni kan i testufa.
Tzeṭṭ deg yiḍ kan acku deg wass ur testufa ara.

D aḍu n tisselbi i d-iṣuḍen di medden!
Taḥnunt ama ad tebḍu, taqjunt ama ad tellaẓ.
Yuli-t ccib n temɣer.
Am acki yebɣa ad yuɣal d aεekkaz ger iḍaṛṛen.
Am ticki yebɣa ad yuɣal d aεekkaz ger iḍaṛṛen.
Am acki ira ad yuɣal d aεekkaz ger iḍaṛṛen.
Am ticki ira ad yuɣal d aεekkaz ger iḍaṛṛen.
Mi yuker ḥedṛeɣ, mi yeggul umneɣ.
Wagi d ibiw n temcict: d win neɣ uhu.
Ur zeddig ara uεeṛqub n yimenεal.
Ur zeddig ara uεeṛqub n yemkerḍen.
Ur zeddig ara uεeṛqub n yir iterrasen.
"Amek aεni tessuṭṭuḍeḍ?" i s-qqaṛen i win yeţḥawalen aseqqi i seksu.
Win yeţḥawalen aseqqi i seksu, qaṛen-as: " Amek aεni tessuṭṭuḍeḍ?"
Zemren ad iɣzifen uḍan af imuḍan.
Teḍra-as am win irekben izem.
Yeṛṛeẓ uqeṛmud af tassaft, yella Rebbi ad d-yerr tiqit.
Anda ar ad tṛuḥeḍ akka?
Anida ar ad tṛuḥeḍ akka?
Anga ar ad tṛuḥeḍ akka?
Anda akka ar ad tṛuḥeḍ?
Azul a wid yestufan, xas ssendu-t aman.
Win yeţţun yedder, yemmut.

Ader, d amyag am bder; maca ader yerna anamek n wawal "ṣubb".
Ader, ṣubb, s tefransist: descendre (ader avec d vélaire).
Mmugreɣ-d yiwen tura hah, acebbub-is yexxeṛčečči; ad as-tiniḍ yemceḍ s tḥeṛṛaqaţ.
Guggin-d qeccuc meccuc: usan-d yakk, s s weqcic s wemcic.
Dduffin-d qeccuc meccuc: usan-d yakk, s warrac-nsen u s yemcac-nsen.
Mmjemmjemen-d qeccuc meccuc : usan-d d tirni, s yeqcicen-nsen u s yemcac-nsen.
Ẓedmen-d qeccuc meccuc : usan-d d tirni, s weqcic-nsen u s wemcic-nsen.
Nneɣlen-d qeccuc meccuc : usan-d d tirni, s uqeccuc-nsen u s wemcic-nsen.
Nneɣlen-d qeccuc meccuc : usan-d d tirni, s weqcic-nsen u s wemcic-nsen.
Mlalen-d qeccuc meccuc : usan-d d tirni, s weqcic-nsen u s wemcic-nsen.
Nneɣlen-d qeccuc meccuc : usan-d d izumal, aqcic taqcict.
Mmarend qeccuc meccuc : usan-d d izumal, aqcic taqcict.
Mmjemmjen-d qeccuc meccuc : usan-d d izumal, aqcic taqcict.
Mmɣen-d qeccuc meccuc: usan-d d izumal, aqcic taqcict.
Mṛuwwaẓen-d qeccuc meccuc: usan-d d izumal, aqcic taqcict.
Mzuεlalen-d qeccuc meccuc: usan-d d tirni, s weqcic s wemcic.
Mǧuεlalen-d qeccuc meccuc: usan-d d tirni, s yeqcicen-nsen u s yemcac-nsen.

Ssneɣ anda i tzedɣeḍ.

Neḥmmel ad necnu.
As-d ɣur-i.
Tewwet lgerra idelli.
Cteddu, d aseṛqes: mara tesjellibeḍ llufan, ad t-teṭṭeqqiṛeḍ ciṭuḥ ar igenni ad tid-teccelqafeḍ. Ilaq ad t-teṭṭfeḍ deg tɣeṛdin mara t-tectettuyeḍ s uḥader. Yezmer ad t-tesṛeqṣeḍ taswiεt azzuh; maca, ma yella yeţru neɣ icax neɣ qelben wallen-is, anef-as ad yesteεfu. Ihi, akka. Tazwara, cituḥ, s leεqel. Mi yuɣ tannumi, d neţţa ara d-yessuturen aseṛqes (acteddu) mara tid-refdeḍ.
Akken i d-inna Thomas Jefferson: "Ma tessunefeḍ cwiṭ n talwit af umud n tlelli, yiwet deg-sent ur ţ-tuklaleḍ."
Yenna-d Thomas Jefferson: "Ma theggaḍ ad tmuddeḍ kra si tlelli-ik d asfel akken ad tidireḍ deg tɣellist, ur tuklaleḍ la ta, la tayeḍ."
Yenna-d Thomas Jefferson: "Ma yella tefkiḍ kra deg tlelli-k d asfel i cwiṭ n talwit, ḥṣu-kid ur tuklaleḍ yiwet."
Yenna-d Thomas Jefferson: "Ma yella tefkiḍ kra deg tlelli-k d asfel i cwiṭ n talwit, ḥṣu-kid ur d-teţḥelliḍ la tin la ta."
Acteddu, cteddu, d aseṛqes: mara tesjellibeḍ llufan, ad t-teṭṭeqqiṛeḍ ciṭuḥ ar igenni ad tid-teccelqafeḍ, mebla ma tserrḥeḍ-as.
Ihi, acteddu, akka. Tazwara, acteddu, cituḥ, s leεqel. Mi yuɣ tannumi, d neţţa ara d-yessuturen aseṛqes akken kan ara tid-refdeḍ.
Deg ucteddu, ilaq agrud-nni ad t-teṭṭfeḍ deg tɣeṛdin mara t-tectettuyeḍ s uḥader. Yezmer ad t-tesṛeqṣeḍ taswiεt azzuh; maca, ma yella yeţru neɣ icax neɣ qelben wallen-is, anef-as ad yesteεfu.
Yenna-d Thomas Jefferson: "Ma teţţaɣeḍ-d talwit-ik s tlelli, ad d-yas wass, ur kid-teţṣiḥi ula d yiwet."
Yenna-d Thomas Jefferson: "Ma yella tefkiḍ d asfel tilelli inek ilmend n talwit, ha-t-an ak-ṛuḥent di snat."
Yeggul deg-i ad eɣzeɣ tasraft i baba, u yerna yuker-iyi agelzim.
Yeggul deg-i ad eɣzeɣ aẓekka i baba, u yerna yeqqed-iyi deg ugelzim.
Talwit-agi, ad d-nuɣal alamma d ɣuṛ-s ayɣeṛ i ţ-nugi: a ḥlili, ma d asfel sɣuṛ tilelli, d aseḥlelli.
Yenna-d Thomas Jefferson: "Ma teǧiḍ cwiṭ di tlelli-k akken akken at-tidireḍ di talwit, kečč  ur tuklaleḍ yiwet seg-sent."
Yenna-d Thomas Jefferson: "Ma tefkiḍ d asfel amud kan si tlelli yinek akken ad tidireḍ war tugdi, di snat yiwet ur da ţ-tuklaked."

Amɣaṛ iceṛhen neɣ iqeṛs-as yinziz, mara yeqqaṛ "cteddu-yi" i umeẓyan neɣ tameẓyant, anamek-is "err-iyid d amejṭuḥ, slef-iyi, iḥnin-d ɣuṛ-i, ssedhu-yi, sduqqes-iyi, atg."
Amɣaṛ iceṛhen neɣ iqeṛs-as yinziz, mara yeqqaṛ "ctettu-yi" i umeẓyan neɣ tameẓyant, anamek-is "err-iyid d amejṭuḥ, slef-iyi, iḥnin-d ɣuṛ-i, ssedhu-yi, sduqqes-iyi, atg."

Ur lliɣ d tameṭṭut.
Nekk d aqcic.
Ɣuṛ-k ha, ad tbeddleḍ ibiw s yeclem!
Yenna-as winna: "Anda-t wudem-iw a lemri?"; yerra-yas-d wayeḍ: "ɣuṛ-k, ahat deffir-s!"
Aserwal awezzlan neqqaṛ-as aqeṛmaḍ. Uqmiẓ, diɣen ticki d ayen izeyyṛen, mačči kan d awezzlan. Yella diɣen uḥmiẓ, aquḍiḍ, uqmiḍ, aqissuḥ.
Yif-it ma yeḥma, la d-qaṛen akka. Wissen imir ma d ayefki, neɣ d inisi. Wamma ziɣ, dacu-t?
La d-qaṛen akka, yif-it ma yeḥma. Wissen imir ma d ayefki, neɣ d inisi. Wamma ziɣ, dacu-t?

Theddṛeḍ tačikit?
Theddṛem tačikit?
Theddṛemt tačikit?
Awid kan tagmaţ ad tugar asluǧǧeḍ.
Awid kan ur ţilint ara rregmat ger watmaten. Wamma wiyiḍ, yeţban anwa-ten mara kid-laεjen.
Dacu ara d-ternum af waya?
Kra iḍeṛṛun akka tura, ad ggalleɣ ur ḥniteɣ yuder-itid Muḥend-u-Yahya deg "Tixurdas n Saεid Weḥsen". Ma mačči ugar.
Kra iḍeṛṛun akka tura, ma mačči ugar,  yuder-itid Muḥend-u-Yahya deg "Tixurdas n Saεid Weḥsen". Ad ggalleɣ ur ḥniteɣ.
Qaṛen s tefransist akked tlaṭinit: "Ammarezg n wid yesteqnaεen s wulac!".
Qaṛen s tefransist akked tlaṭinit: "Ammarezg n wid yeţţellin kan iqemmac-nsen!".
Qaṛen s tefransist akked tlaṭinit: "Ammarezg n wid yesseblaεen izan!".
Qaṛen s tefransist akked tlaṭinit: "Ammarezg n wid ur neẓri anda tedda taluft!".
Qaṛen s tefransist akked tlaṭinit: "Ammarezg n wid ur neẓri anwa i tent-iɣezzlen!".
Qaṛen s tefransist akked tlaṭinit: "Ammarezg n wid ur neẓri anda i tent-uɣen!".
Qaṛen s tefransist akked tlaṭinit: "Ammarezg n wid ur neẓri anda i ṭeṛḍiqent!".
Inissi, deffir imensi!
Asensi yusa-d deg umyag "ens" ideg i d-kksen imensi (ayen ara nečč send ad nens, ad neṭṭes); ula d isem n temdint n Tunes, anda ţnusun iserdasen Imaziɣen. Ad d-nernu: mara tessexsiḍ times, dɣa times-nni ad tens. Akka, neɣ aha°?
